As for nontrivial process
\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}\not=0
, we find that (
E.69
) is solved by
l_{{\rm 12}}^{\#}=0
and (
E.70
) – by
l^{\#i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}i_{4}}=0
(he simplest way to find this latter solution is to use SO(8) frame in which

{K}^{=i}_{{\rm 12}}=|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|\,\delta^{i}_{8}
), so that

l_{{\rm 1}q}^{+\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}p}^{+\underline{q}}=0
.

So far
we have proved that for the nontrivial process involving one massive and two massless (super-)particles
the coefficients in the decomposition (
5.97
) of massive helicity spinors over two massless helicity spinors
obey the relations

\displaystyle l_{{\rm 1}q}^{+\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 1}p}^{+\underline{q}}=2l_{{\rm 1}}^{\#}\delta_{qp}\;,\qquad{}\qquad l_{{\rm 1}q}^{+\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}p}^{+\underline{q}}=0\;,\qquad{}\qquad l_{{\rm 2}q}^{+\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}p}^{+\underline{q}}=2l_{{\rm 2}}^{\#}\delta_{qp}\;.\qquad

(E.71)

It is convenient to define
l_{{\rm i}q}^{\;\underline{q}}:=\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{2l_{{\rm 1}}^{\#}}}\,l_{{\rm i}q}^{+\underline{q}}
(for simplicity we restrict our consideration by the case of
l_{{\rm 1}}^{\#}>0
which corresponds to
\rho_{{\rm 1}}^{\#}<0
, see (
5.110
))
which obey

\displaystyle l_{{\rm 1}q}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 1}p}^{\;\underline{q}}=\delta_{qp}\;,\qquad{}\qquad l_{{\rm 1}q}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}p}^{\;\underline{q}}=0\;,\qquad{}\qquad l_{{\rm 2}q}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}p}^{\;\underline{q}}=\delta_{qp}\;\qquad

(E.72)

and hence form the SO(16) valued matrix

\displaystyle\left(\,l_{{\rm 1}q}^{\;\underline{q}}\,,\,l_{{\rm 2}q}^{\;\underline{q}}\right)\quad\in\quad O(16)\;.

(E.73)

This also implies

\displaystyle\delta_{\underline{q}\underline{p}}=l_{{\rm 1}p}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 1}p}^{\;\underline{p}}+l_{{\rm 2}p}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}p}^{\;\underline{p}}\;.\qquad

(E.74)

In this terms the decomposition (
5.97
) of the helicity spinor of the massive superparticle in the case of the the three point process becomes

\displaystyle{\rm v}_{\alpha}{}^{\underline{q}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2l_{{\rm 1}}^{\#}}v_{\alpha q{\rm 1}}^{\;-}l_{q{\rm 1}}^{\;\underline{q}}+\sqrt{2l_{{\rm 2}}^{\#}}v_{\alpha{q}{\rm 2}}^{\;-}l_{{q}{\rm 2}}^{\;\underline{q}}\;.\qquad

(E.75)

Notice that for inverse spinor moving frame matrix of the massive superparticle in 3 point process we can obtain an expression similar to (
E.75
) in the same way as (
E.75
) but from momentum conservation (
5.109
) multiplied by
\tilde{\sigma}_{\mu}^{\alpha\beta}
,

\displaystyle{\rm v}_{\underline{q}}{}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2l_{{\rm 1}}^{\#}}v^{-\alpha}_{\dot{q}{\rm 1}}l_{\dot{q}{\rm 1}}^{\;\underline{q}}+\sqrt{2l_{{\rm 2}}^{\#}}v^{-\alpha}_{\dot{q}{\rm 2}}l_{\dot{q}{\rm 2}}^{\;\underline{q}}\;\qquad

(E.76)

with
l_{\dot{q}{\rm 1,2}}^{\;\underline{q}}
also forming

O(16)
matrix, which is to say, obeying

\displaystyle l_{{\rm 1}\dot{q}}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 1}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{q}}=\delta_{\dot{q}\dot{p}}\;,\qquad{}\qquad l_{{\rm 1}\dot{q}}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{q}}=0\;,\qquad{}\qquad l_{{\rm 2}\dot{q}}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{q}}=\delta_{\dot{q}\dot{p}}\;,\qquad

(E.77)

\displaystyle\delta_{\underline{q}\underline{p}}=l_{{\rm 1}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 1}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{p}}+l_{{\rm 2}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{q}}\,l_{{\rm 2}\dot{p}}^{\;\underline{p}}\;.\qquad

(E.78)

The relation between
l_{\dot{q}{\rm 1,2}}^{\;\underline{q}}
and
l_{{q}{\rm 1,2}}^{\;\underline{q}}
can be then obtained from that (
E.76
) is inverse to (
E.75
):

\displaystyle l_{\dot{q}{\rm 2}}^{\;\underline{q}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-\,\frac{1}{2\sqrt{l^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}l^{\#}_{{\rm 2}}}\,(\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}})^{2}}\,\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}\,l_{{q}{\rm 1}}^{\;\underline{q}}\vec{\gamma}_{q\dot{q}}\;,\qquad

(E.79)

\displaystyle l_{\dot{q}{\rm 1}}^{\;\underline{q}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{2\sqrt{l^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}l^{\#}_{{\rm 2}}}\,(\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}})^{2}}\,\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}\,l_{{q}{\rm 2}}^{\;\underline{q}}\vec{\gamma}_{q\dot{q}}=\,-\,\frac{1}{2\sqrt{l^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}l^{\#}_{{\rm 2}}}\,(\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}})^{2}}\,\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 12}}\,l_{{q}{\rm 2}}^{\;\underline{q}}\vec{\gamma}_{q\dot{q}}\;,\qquad

(E.80)

E.2
Three point amplitude with D
0
-brane

Let us use our results on three particle kinematics to calculate the first multiplier in (
5.113
), namely the second rank SO(9) tensor
p_{{\rm 1}\mu}{\rm u}^{\mu I}_{{\rm 3}}p_{{\rm 2}\nu}{\rm u}^{\nu J}_{{\rm 3}}
:

\displaystyle p_{{\rm 1}\mu}{\rm u}^{\mu I}_{{\rm 3}}p_{{\rm 2}\nu}{\rm u}^{\nu J}_{{\rm 3}}=\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{16{\rm m}^{2}}\,<2^{-}_{q}|1^{-}_{\dot{q}}><2^{-}_{p}|1^{-}_{\dot{q}}>\,l^{\underline{q}}_{q{\rm 2}}\gamma^{I}_{\underline{q}\underline{p}}l^{\underline{p}}_{p{\rm 2}}\,\,<1^{-}_{r}|2^{-}_{\dot{s}}><1^{-}_{s}|2^{-}_{\dot{s}}>\,l^{\underline{r}}_{r{\rm 1}}\gamma^{J}_{\underline{r}\underline{s}}l^{\underline{s}}_{s{\rm 1}}\,

\displaystyle=\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{64{\rm m}^{2}}\,|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|^{4}\,l^{\underline{q}}_{q{\rm 2}}\gamma^{I}_{\underline{q}\underline{p}}l^{\underline{p}}_{q{\rm 2}}\,\,\,l^{\underline{r}}_{p{\rm 1}}\gamma^{J}_{\underline{r}\underline{s}}l^{\underline{s}}_{p{\rm 1}}\,.

(E.81)

To obtain the first equality we have used Eqs.
(
5.92
), (
5.111
) and
p_{{\rm i}\mu}=\frac{1}{8}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm i}}v_{{\rm i}\dot{q}}^{-}{\sigma}_{\mu}v_{{\rm i}\dot{q}}^{-}
, for
{\rm i}=1,2
, which follows from (
5.1
) and
{\rm i}
–th counterpart of (
A.3
).
The second equality uses (
5.9
) and algebra of
SO(8)
counterparts of Pauli matrices, (
2.16
).

Then, using
(
5.104
)–(
5.106
), we find

\displaystyle p_{{\rm 1}\mu}{\rm u}^{\mu I}_{{\rm 3}}p_{{\rm 2}\mu}{\rm u}^{\mu J}_{{\rm 3}}U_{(I}\bar{U}_{J)}=\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{128{\rm m}^{2}}\,|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|^{4}\,\left((l_{q{\rm 2}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{A}){\bar{\cal U}}{}^{AB}(l_{q{\rm 2}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\;(l_{p{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{C})\,{\cal U}{}_{CD}(l_{p{\rm 1}}){\rm w}^{D}+{\rm 1}\leftrightarrow{\rm 2})\,\right)\qquad

\displaystyle=-\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{64{\rm m}^{2}}\,|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|^{4}\,\left|(l_{q{\rm 2}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{A}){\bar{\cal U}}{}^{AB}(l_{q{\rm 2}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\;\right|^{2}=-\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{64{\rm m}^{2}}\,|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|^{4}\,\left|(l_{q{\rm 1}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{A}){\bar{\cal U}}{}^{AB}(l_{q{\rm 1}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\;\right|^{2}\;,\qquad

(E.82)

where
(l_{q{\rm i}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{A})=l_{q{\rm i}}^{\;\underline{q}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{\underline{q}A}=(l_{q{\rm 2}}{\rm w}^{A})^{*}
and, to arrive at the final expression, we have used

(l_{q{\rm 2}}{\rm w}^{A})\,(l_{q{\rm 2}}{\rm w}^{B})=-(l_{q{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{A})\,(l_{q{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{B})

(E.83)

and its c.c. These as well as

(l_{q{\rm 2}}{\rm w}^{A})\,(l_{q{\rm 2}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\,(\sigma^{(\check{I}|}{\bar{\cal U}})_{A}{}^{B}=-(l_{q{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{A})\,(l_{q{\rm 1}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\,(\sigma^{(\check{I}|}{\bar{\cal U}})_{A}{}^{B}

(E.84)

are correct, in their turn, due to (
5.88
)
as well as to Eq. (
E.74
) and tracelessness of
(\sigma^{\check{J}}{\bar{\cal U}})_{C}{}^{D}
.

Similarly, using (
E.84
), we obtain

\displaystyle p_{{\rm 1}\mu}{\rm u}^{\mu I}_{{\rm 3}}p_{{\rm 2}\mu}{\rm u}^{\mu J}_{{\rm 3}}U_{(I}^{\check{I}}U_{J)}^{\check{J}}=-\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{16{\rm m}^{2}}\,|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|^{4}\,(l_{q{\rm 2}}{\rm w}^{A})\,(l_{q{\rm 2}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\,(\sigma^{(\check{I}|}{\bar{\cal U}})_{A}{}^{B}\;(l_{q{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{C})\,(l_{q{\rm 1}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{D})\,(\sigma^{|\check{J})}{\bar{\cal U}})_{C}{}^{D}\qquad

\displaystyle=+\frac{(\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 1}}\rho^{\#}_{{\rm 2}})^{2}}{16{\rm m}^{2}}\,|\vec{K}^{=}_{{\rm 21}}|^{4}\,(l_{q{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{A})\,(l_{q{\rm 1}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{B})\,(\sigma^{\check{I}}{\bar{\cal U}})_{A}{}^{B}\;(l_{q{\rm 1}}{\rm w}^{C})\,(l_{q{\rm 1}}\bar{{\rm w}}_{D})\,(\sigma^{\check{J}}{\bar{\cal U}})_{C}{}^{D}\qquad

(E.85)

In such a way we have found the expression (
5.114
) for the amplitude (
5.113
).
